This is the player to be shown a card model — the live BACK/LAY market — not match totals. Confirmed starters only, rebuilt each Monday on the trailing 365 days of card logs, then team-normalised to that week’s team-cards home/away means. 1 unit at the best TheStatsAPI player_booked quote (Bet365 / Paddy Power / BetMGM). Low-confidence backs are excluded, matching the live sheet. TSA only returned player-booked prices for 15 Aug–6 Sep (133 of 603 games; Championship had none). After that the player-odds endpoint 403s, so later weeks are calibration only.

What “passed the BACK test” means

A starter passes when all of these hold: outfield, team-normalised, TSA price present, model confidence high or medium, and BACK EV at least the confidence threshold (high = 7% with ≥30 starts; medium = 10% with ≥10 starts). Everything else is did not pass. Low confidence (fewer than 10 starts, transfers, stale, or prior-only) is never a BACK on the live sheet, even at a fat price.
